# Unit 22 linguistic and owned-edition mapping packet

This packet prepares review. It records an unsigned structural mapping proposal. It does not record a qualified review, an approved edition alignment, or an install decision.

The owned-edition mapping worksheet separates observed edition facts, the proposed lesson and printed page range, unresolved edition questions, and matters that still require qualified linguistic review. ISBN, owned-copy verification, variance, reviewer identity, qualification, date, and approval stay blank. The disposition remains PENDING_CURRICULUM_REVIEWER.

Unit 22 remains in development. Its readiness is content-complete and review-pending. The Lesson 22 relationship is a proposed, unsigned mapping. It is not an approved edition alignment.

`mappingPackageDigest` binds the proposed edition-mapping facts for Unit 22: edition identity, lesson, printed page range, topic description, observation source, ISBN observation state, and the pending disposition. Reviewer identity, qualification, date, notes, correction notes, and signature stay outside that digest.

`packContentReviewDigest` is the repository aggregate of instructional curriculum. It is not an approval of this unit, and it is not part of the review-package digest.

`reviewPackageDigest` identifies this complete pending review package. It binds the ordered instructional records and the mapping-package digest. Changing a proposed page range or topic changes the mapping-package digest and therefore this package digest.

`repositorySourceDigest` binds repository and release inputs. Recording an approval later changes that repository hash. That change does not replace the signed content-review digest or the mapping-package digest.

The marker scan used by this package detects only a fixed list of known prohibited mapping-input markers. It is not comprehensive copyright detection and cannot recognize every copy or close paraphrase of protected material.

recordType=meaning
sourceUrl=https://en.wikipedia.org/w/index.php?title=Russian_grammar&oldid=1369617627
sourceRevision=1369617627
sourceRecordId=grammar:several classes of numerals ([имена] числительные): cardinal, ordinal, collectiv
originalSourceText=several classes of numerals ([имена] числительные): cardinal, ordinal, collective, and also fractional constructions; also it has other types of words, relative to numbers: collective adverbial forms (вдвоём), multiplicative (двойной) and counting-system (двоичный) adjectives, some numeric-pronominal and indefinite quantity words (сколько, много, несколько). Here are the numerals from 0 to 10:

{| class="wikitable"
!
! cardinal numbers
! ordinal numbers<br />(nominative case, masculine)
! collective numbers
|-
! 0
| ноль ''or'' нуль || нулево́й || rowspan="2" | —
|-
! 1
| оди́н ({{abbr|m.|masculine}}), одна́ ({{abbr|f.|feminine}}), одно́ ({{abbr|n.|neuter}}), одни́ ({{abbr|pl.|plural}})<br />(раз may be used when counting, a colloquial option) || пе́рвый 
|-
! 2
| два ({{abbr|m.|masculine}}, {{abbr|n.|neuter}}), две ({{abbr|f.|feminine}}) || второ́й || дво́е
|-
! 3
| три || тре́тий || тро́е
|-
! 4
| четы́ре || четвёртый || че́тверо
|-
! 5
| пять || пя́тый || пя́теро
|-
! 6
| шесть || шесто́й || ше́стеро
|-
! 7
| семь || седьмо́й || се́меро
|-
! 8
| во́семь || восьмо́й || (во́сьмеро)<ref>Collective numerals for more than 7 are seldom used.</ref>
|-
! 9
| де́вять || девя́тый || (де́вятеро)
|-
! 10
| де́сять || деся́тый || (де́сятеро)
|}
shippedText=дво́е
transformation=selection
licenseId=CC BY-SA 4.0
licenseUrl=https://creativecommons.org/licenses/by-sa/4.0/
attribution=Wikipedia contributors, CC BY-SA 4.0, via Wikipedia:Copyrights oldid 1369466282. Page history for oldid 1369617627 identifies the contributors.
shareAlikeTreatment=Adapted curriculum data, selection, and arrangement are distributed under CC BY-SA 4.0. Application code, user data, and user recordings are outside that statement. No audio is relicensed because this unit ships no audio.
sourceHash=5da558630e9ee6f8d46e13b4a8485e3cc595f337b419ecec32a50085ebc99b8a
contentHash=f5723d0db33e785f366cb301f56a24865ac28d15a77706e835f5b320d29e8efc

originalSourceText=several classes of numerals ([имена] числительные): cardinal, ordinal, collective, and also fractional constructions; also it has other types of words, relative to numbers: collective adverbial forms (вдвоём), multiplicative (двойной) and counting-system (двоичный) adjectives, some numeric-pronominal and indefinite quantity words (сколько, много, несколько). Here are the numerals from 0 to 10:

{| class="wikitable"
!
! cardinal numbers
! ordinal numbers<br />(nominative case, masculine)
! collective numbers
|-
! 0
| ноль ''or'' нуль || нулево́й || rowspan="2" | —
|-
! 1
| оди́н ({{abbr|m.|masculine}}), одна́ ({{abbr|f.|feminine}}), одно́ ({{abbr|n.|neuter}}), одни́ ({{abbr|pl.|plural}})<br />(раз may be used when counting, a colloquial option) || пе́рвый 
|-
! 2
| два ({{abbr|m.|masculine}}, {{abbr|n.|neuter}}), две ({{abbr|f.|feminine}}) || второ́й || дво́е
|-
! 3
| три || тре́тий || тро́е
|-
! 4
| четы́ре || четвёртый || че́тверо
|-
! 5
| пять || пя́тый || пя́теро
|-
! 6
| шесть || шесто́й || ше́стеро
|-
! 7
| семь || седьмо́й || се́меро
|-
! 8
| во́семь || восьмо́й || (во́сьмеро)<ref>Collective numerals for more than 7 are seldom used.</ref>
|-
! 9
| де́вять || девя́тый || (де́вятеро)
|-
! 10
| де́сять || деся́тый || (де́сятеро)
|}
shippedText=четы́ре
transformation=selection

shareAlikeTreatment=Adapted curriculum data, selection, and arrangement are distributed under CC BY-SA 4.0. Application code, user data, and user recordings are outside that statement. No audio is relicensed because this unit ships no audio.

originalSourceText=Numerals larger than twenty follow the above rules using the last digit.  Think of numerals ending in zero as ending in ten.  For example:  тридцать три рюмки (thirty-three shot glasses), сорок один год (forty-one years), шестьдесят девять слов (sixty-nine words).

recordType=meaning
sourceUrl=https://en.wikibooks.org/wiki/Russian/Numbers?oldid=4371358
sourceRevision=4371358
sourceRecordId=numbers:The numeral один (1) should be used in the proper form to indicate gender (and, 
originalSourceText=The numeral один (1) should be used in the proper form to indicate gender (and, number, believe it or not).  For example: один мальчик (one boy), одна девушка (one girl), одно животное (one animal), одни джинсы (one [pair of] jeans).  The plural form of the numeral (1) is a bit of an oddity, but its use will become clear with time.  Following a form of один should come the item at hand in the nominative or inanimate accusative case, as appropriate.

The numerals два (or две), три, and четыре all require the following item to be presented in the genitive singular form.  The numeral два has two forms: два for masculine and neuter items and две for feminine items.  After the numeral два, each numeral has only a single form.  See for example:  две девушки (two girls), три кошки (three cats), четыре мальчика (four boys).  

The numerals пять (5) through двадцать (20) require their attached items in the genitive plural forms, as in these examples:  пять девушек (five girls), десять копеек (ten kopeks), шестнадцать рублей (sixteen rubles).
shippedText=one boy
transformation=selection

recordType=meaning
sourceUrl=https://en.wikibooks.org/wiki/Russian/Numbers?oldid=4371358
sourceRevision=4371358
sourceRecordId=numbers:The numeral один (1) should be used in the proper form to indicate gender (and, 
originalSourceText=The numeral один (1) should be used in the proper form to indicate gender (and, number, believe it or not).  For example: один мальчик (one boy), одна девушка (one girl), одно животное (one animal), одни джинсы (one [pair of] jeans).  The plural form of the numeral (1) is a bit of an oddity, but its use will become clear with time.  Following a form of один should come the item at hand in the nominative or inanimate accusative case, as appropriate.

The numerals два (or две), три, and четыре all require the following item to be presented in the genitive singular form.  The numeral два has two forms: два for masculine and neuter items and две for feminine items.  After the numeral два, each numeral has only a single form.  See for example:  две девушки (two girls), три кошки (three cats), четыре мальчика (four boys).  

The numerals пять (5) through двадцать (20) require their attached items in the genitive plural forms, as in these examples:  пять девушек (five girls), десять копеек (ten kopeks), шестнадцать рублей (sixteen rubles).
shippedText=ten kopeks

recordType=instruction
sourceUrl=https://en.wikipedia.org/w/index.php?title=Russian_grammar&oldid=1369617627
sourceRevision=1369617627
sourceRecordId=grammar:several classes of numerals ([имена] числительные): cardinal, ordinal, collectiv
originalSourceText=several classes of numerals ([имена] числительные): cardinal, ordinal, collective, and also fractional constructions; also it has other types of words, relative to numbers: collective adverbial forms (вдвоём), multiplicative (двойной) and counting-system (двоичный) adjectives, some numeric-pronominal and indefinite quantity words (сколько, много, несколько). Here are the numerals from 0 to 10:

{| class="wikitable"
!
! cardinal numbers
! ordinal numbers<br />(nominative case, masculine)
! collective numbers
|-
! 0
| ноль ''or'' нуль || нулево́й || rowspan="2" | —
|-
! 1
| оди́н ({{abbr|m.|masculine}}), одна́ ({{abbr|f.|feminine}}), одно́ ({{abbr|n.|neuter}}), одни́ ({{abbr|pl.|plural}})<br />(раз may be used when counting, a colloquial option) || пе́рвый 
|-
! 2
| два ({{abbr|m.|masculine}}, {{abbr|n.|neuter}}), две ({{abbr|f.|feminine}}) || второ́й || дво́е
|-
! 3
| три || тре́тий || тро́е
|-
! 4
| четы́ре || четвёртый || че́тверо
|-
! 5
| пять || пя́тый || пя́теро
|-
! 6
| шесть || шесто́й || ше́стеро
|-
! 7
| семь || седьмо́й || се́меро
|-
! 8
| во́семь || восьмо́й || (во́сьмеро)<ref>Collective numerals for more than 7 are seldom used.</ref>
|-
! 9
| де́вять || девя́тый || (де́вятеро)
|-
! 10
| де́сять || деся́тый || (де́сятеро)
|}
shippedText=collective numbers
transformation=selection
licenseId=CC BY-SA 4.0
licenseUrl=https://creativecommons.org/licenses/by-sa/4.0/
attribution=Wikipedia contributors, CC BY-SA 4.0, via Wikipedia:Copyrights oldid 1369466282. Page history for oldid 1369617627 identifies the contributors.
shareAlikeTreatment=Adapted curriculum data, selection, and arrangement are distributed under CC BY-SA 4.0. Application code, user data, and user recordings are outside that statement. No audio is relicensed because this unit ships no audio.
sourceHash=5da558630e9ee6f8d46e13b4a8485e3cc595f337b419ecec32a50085ebc99b8a
contentHash=f8156090998accef456c2f179558dd3bc6acac87f2a2f50e4d2d6b043be27474
audioAssetId=

reviewDate=

recordType=label
sourceUrl=https://en.wikipedia.org/w/index.php?title=Russian_grammar&oldid=1369617627
sourceRevision=1369617627
sourceRecordId=grammar:several classes of numerals ([имена] числительные): cardinal, ordinal, collectiv
originalSourceText=several classes of numerals ([имена] числительные): cardinal, ordinal, collective, and also fractional constructions; also it has other types of words, relative to numbers: collective adverbial forms (вдвоём), multiplicative (двойной) and counting-system (двоичный) adjectives, some numeric-pronominal and indefinite quantity words (сколько, много, несколько). Here are the numerals from 0 to 10:

{| class="wikitable"
!
! cardinal numbers
! ordinal numbers<br />(nominative case, masculine)
! collective numbers
|-
! 0
| ноль ''or'' нуль || нулево́й || rowspan="2" | —
|-
! 1
| оди́н ({{abbr|m.|masculine}}), одна́ ({{abbr|f.|feminine}}), одно́ ({{abbr|n.|neuter}}), одни́ ({{abbr|pl.|plural}})<br />(раз may be used when counting, a colloquial option) || пе́рвый 
|-
! 2
| два ({{abbr|m.|masculine}}, {{abbr|n.|neuter}}), две ({{abbr|f.|feminine}}) || второ́й || дво́е
|-
! 3
| три || тре́тий || тро́е
|-
! 4
| четы́ре || четвёртый || че́тверо
|-
! 5
| пять || пя́тый || пя́теро
|-
! 6
| шесть || шесто́й || ше́стеро
|-
! 7
| семь || седьмо́й || се́меро
|-
! 8
| во́семь || восьмо́й || (во́сьмеро)<ref>Collective numerals for more than 7 are seldom used.</ref>
|-
! 9
| де́вять || девя́тый || (де́вятеро)
|-
! 10
| де́сять || деся́тый || (де́сятеро)
|}

recordType=instruction
sourceUrl=https://en.wikibooks.org/wiki/Russian/Numbers?oldid=4371358
sourceRevision=4371358
sourceRecordId=numbers:Numerals larger than twenty follow the above rules using the last digit.  Think 
originalSourceText=Numerals larger than twenty follow the above rules using the last digit.  Think of numerals ending in zero as ending in ten.  For example:  тридцать три рюмки (thirty-three shot glasses), сорок один год (forty-one years), шестьдесят девять слов (sixty-nine words).

recordType=instruction
sourceUrl=https://en.wikibooks.org/wiki/Russian/Numbers?oldid=4371358
sourceRevision=4371358
sourceRecordId=numbers:The numeral один (1) should be used in the proper form to indicate gender (and, 
originalSourceText=The numeral один (1) should be used in the proper form to indicate gender (and, number, believe it or not).  For example: один мальчик (one boy), одна девушка (one girl), одно животное (one animal), одни джинсы (one [pair of] jeans).  The plural form of the numeral (1) is a bit of an oddity, but its use will become clear with time.  Following a form of один should come the item at hand in the nominative or inanimate accusative case, as appropriate.

The numerals два (or две), три, and четыре all require the following item to be presented in the genitive singular form.  The numeral два has two forms: два for masculine and neuter items and две for feminine items.  After the numeral два, each numeral has only a single form.  See for example:  две девушки (two girls), три кошки (three cats), четыре мальчика (four boys).  

The numerals пять (5) through двадцать (20) require their attached items in the genitive plural forms, as in these examples:  пять девушек (five girls), десять копеек (ten kopeks), шестнадцать рублей (sixteen rubles).
shippedText=The numeral один (1) should be used in the proper form to indicate gender (and, number, believe it or not).
